- **capture**: Brain-dump organizer. Ingests an unstructured stream of mixed thoughts, tasks, ideas and transforms it into a clean four-section actionable system (Projects/Ideas, Tasks, Connections, How I Can Help) with zero information loss. Fast-to-action by design — no upfront intake. Asks at most one mid-organization clarifying question when a single item is genuinely ambiguous between task and project. Workspace detection is real (Glob/Grep) — never fabricates connections. Source spec: megaprompts/05-capture-megaprompt.md (PR #657). - **cc-enforcer**: Rules your coding agent physically cannot ignore. A Claude Code plugin (and LLM-agnostic rule pack) that stops reactive patching, guessed citations, surface-level fixes and premature "done" claims by intercepting the agent's own tool calls rather than asking it nicely. PreToolUse denies an edit before it lands — editing a file you never opened this session, a suppression marker with no adjacent why-comment, a hardcoded secret or user-home path, the fourth small patch to one file, a dangerous shell flag. A nine-layer Stop gate then refuses to let a completion claim end the turn without evidenc… ([skymanbp/cc-enforcer](https://github.com/skymanbp/cc-enforcer))

- **cc-fancy-html-hook**: Claude Code가 .md 파일을 Write/Edit하면 일반 HTML과 인포그래픽 스타일 '팬시 HTML'을 자동 생성해 브라우저로 띄우는 PostToolUse hook. python-markdown으로 일반본을 즉시 변환, 백그라운드 `claude -p` 서브 프로세스로 팬시본을 비동기 생성. 매번 다이얼로그로 생성 여부를 묻는 대화형. macOS(Bash + AppleScript) + Windows(PowerShell + WinForms) 양쪽 hook 자동 등록. - **ccpm**: Lean Project Management for solo developers managing multiple projects. 19 optimized commands, 50-60% token reduction via smart caching, natural workflow (plan, work, sync, commit, verify, done), tool-agnostic architecture supporting any PM system via MCP (Linear, Jira, Confluence, etc.), intelligent agent auto-invocation, interactive planning with deep research, monorepo support, safety-first design.
